- **security-expert**: &gt; **Do not rename this file to `security.md`.** OpenSSF Scorecard finds the &gt; repository's security policy by filename, and its path matcher falls back to &gt; comparing the **basename** — so any `security.md` anywhere in the tree is a &gt; candidate. It then parses the first match it encounters and stops.
